The file system, process output,\n"},{"number":910,"text":"screen logs, worker artifacts and product files are observations. They\n"},{"number":911,"text":"become authority only after admission into typed evidence and their fold\n"},{"number":912,"text":"into ledger and decision truth (P6, P3).\n"},{"number":913,"text":"\n"},{"number":914,"text":"A1a Workspace-ledger governed work pair. The worksite or workspace and the\n"},{"number":915,"text":"ledger form the governed work pair (P3, P4b):\n"},{"number":916,"text":"\n"},{"number":917,"text":"```text\n"},{"number":918,"text":"W = mutable workspace under construction\n"},{"number":919,"text":"L = immutable governed ledger of work over W\n"},{"number":920,"text":"E = immutable event log / replay spine\n"},{"number":921,"text":"Ev = evaluator work over L\n"},{"number":922,"text":"```\n"},{"number":923,"text":"\n"},{"number":924,"text":"The workspace is where F_P construction happens. The ledger is the\n"},{"number":925,"text":"immutable governed account of closure-relevant work over that workspace;\n"},{"number":926,"text":"it is not a copy of the workspace but the admitted trace of callout basis,\n"},{"number":927,"text":"observed deltas, file refs, digests, execution evidence, obligations\n"},{"number":928,"text":"touched, claims made, evaluator inputs, evaluator outputs and admission\n"},{"number":929,"text":"status. The loop is (P3, P6):\n"},{"number":930,"text":"\n"},{"number":931,"text":"```text\n"},{"number":932,"text":"F_P acts in W.\n"},{"number":933,"text":"Admission records governed facts in L.\n"},{"number":934,"text":"E orders and anchors L entries with predecessor refs.\n"},{"number":935,"text":"Ev evaluates a declared L snapshot, not ambient W.\n"},{"number":936,"text":"Ev output is itself F_P work and is admitted back into L/E.\n"},{"number":937,"text":"L/E constrain the next lawful F_P action in W.\n"},{"number":938,"text":"```\n"},{"number":939,"text":"\n"},{"number":940,"text":"The consistency rule is (P3, P6):\n"},{"number":941,"text":"\n"},{"number":942,"text":"```text\n"},{"number":943,"text":"W can change without being true.\n"},{"number":944,"text":"L can be true without being the whole W.\n"},{"number":945,"text":"Closure can only use W through L.\n"},{"number":946,"text":"Eval can only judge W through L.\n"},{"number":947,"text":"Eval only affects traversal after its own L/E admission.\n"},{"number":948,"text":"```\n"},{"number":949,"text":"\n"},{"number":950,"text":"Every closure-relevant F_P callout that does work needs ledger admission.\n"},{"number":951,"text":"Evaluator work is not outside F_P merely because it reads ledgers: it is\n"},{"number":952,"text":"F_P work over governed ledger state, and its result must be admitted into\n"},{"number":953,"text":"the immutable ledger and event spine before it can affect closure, routing\n"},{"number":954,"text":"or traversal. Corrections append or supersede ledger and event facts; they\n"},{"number":955,"text":"do not rewrite the governed history of work (P4b, P6, P3).\n"},{"number":956,"text":"\n"},{"number":957,"text":"A1b Ledger is governed attention. A ledger is the system's governed\n"},{"number":958,"text":"attention over construction history. This is a structural analogy and not\n"},{"number":959,"text":"an implementation dependency on transformer attention: model attention\n"},{"number":960,"text":"selects from context through learned weights, while ODD ledger attention\n"},{"number":961,"text":"is typed, immutable, lineage-constrained, policy-bounded and replayable.\n"},{"number":962,"text":"An evaluator may attend only to admitted ledger rows reachable through\n"},{"number":963,"text":"declared lineage, predecessor refs, target binding, policy and the\n"},{"number":964,"text":"relevant event-log cursor. Workspace facts, worker prose, archives,\n"},{"number":965,"text":"summaries and controller memory outside that reachable ledger set lie\n"},{"number":966,"text":"outside the evaluator's lawful attention surface. Lineage is therefore\n"},{"number":967,"text":"not only provenance bookkeeping: it is the attention graph that decides\n"},{"number":968,"text":"which evidence the evaluator may see, what it must ignore, and which\n"},{"number":969,"text":"predecessor chain makes the judgment reproducible (P0, P6, P3).\n"},{"number":970,"text":"\n"},{"number":971,"text":"A2 Exact target binding. Every constructive action must bind the current\n"},{"number":972,"text":"gap to exact target assets and obligations before invocation (P2, P3):\n"},{"number":973,"text":"\n"},{"number":974,"text":"```text\n"},{"number":975,"text":"selected Product outcome ref\n"},{"number":976,"text":"+ optional admitted prerequisite or experiment basis ref\n"},{"number":977,"text":"+ gap pressure + target assets + required roles + evidence refs\n"},{"number":978,"text":"-> TargetObligationBinding\n"},{"number":979,"text":"```\n"},{"number":980,"text":"\n"},{"number":981,"text":"A broad edge cannot satisfy a specific product or evidence gap unless the\n"},{"number":982,"text":"binding proves that edge is the lawful action for those exact assets\n"},{"number":983,"text":"(P2).\n"},{"number":984,"text":"\n"},{"number":985,"text":"A3 Published action law. `evaluate_next` may rank only published lawful\n"},{"number":986,"text":"actions from the action catalogue. If the desired action is unpublished,\n"},{"number":987,"text":"the lawful result is a typed no-action disposition, not a fallback to a\n"},{"number":988,"text":"broad executive graph or a local script (P6, P5).\n"},{"number":989,"text":"\n"},{"number":990,"text":"A4 `evaluate_next` totality. `evaluate_next` must be total. On\n"},{"number":991,"text":"`initial_selection` it selects from the current model, current gap, target\n"},{"number":992,"text":"binding, catalogue and policy. On `post_yield_resume` it resumes or yields\n"},{"number":993,"text":"the same edge from replay-visible resume truth without classifying the\n"},{"number":994,"text":"action as failure. On `post_close_graph_continuation` it may select the\n"},{"number":995,"text":"next graph edge after the current edge closed, or no action when the graph\n"},{"number":996,"text":"is complete. On `post_retry`, `post_repair`, `post_reenter` and\n"},{"number":997,"text":"`post_block` it follows this rule (P5, P6):\n"},{"number":998,"text":"\n"},{"number":999,"text":"```text\n"},{"number":1000,"text":"if higher-priority lawful action exists:\n"},{"number":1001,"text":"  select it\n"},{"number":1002,"text":"else if current graph edge remains lawful:\n"},{"number":1003,"text":"  follow the graph\n"},{"number":1004,"text":"else if authority is missing:\n"},{"number":1005,"text":"  reprice or block\n"},{"number":1006,"text":"else:\n"},{"number":1007,"text":"  block with typed no-action disposition\n"},{"number":1008,"text":"```\n"},{"number":1009,"text":"\n"},{"number":1010,"text":"On `post_reprice`, `evaluate_next` must return a typed no-action\n"},{"number":1011,"text":"disposition while constitutional re-entry stays unresolved. The reprice\n"},{"number":1012,"text":"decision cannot authorise a replacement action; work may be evaluated\n"},{"number":1013,"text":"again only after lawful re-entry separately admits an exact selected\n"},{"number":1014,"text":"Product outcome or bounded basis and supplies it through a new\n"},{"number":1015,"text":"`initial_selection` or a basis-bearing resume. Default graph following is\n"},{"number":1016,"text":"ordinary, but it is still an `evaluate_next` decision and not hidden\n"},{"number":1017,"text":"sequential control (P5, P2, P6).\n"},{"number":1018,"text":"\n"},{"number":1019,"text":"Selection and its results. `evaluate_next`'s selection of what is\n"},{"number":1020,"text":"invoked next is invocational data (A11) and is not itself a value of the\n"},{"number":1021,"text":"core result algebra of\n"},{"number":1022,"text":"[FOUNDATIONS §4 Results](FOUNDATIONS.md#results). A typed no-action\n"},{"number":1023,"text":"disposition, however, records a material determination: that no\n"},{"number":1024,"text":"published lawful action can be selected. Each typed no-action returns its\n"},{"number":1025,"text":"own result for the predicate \"a published lawful action can be selected\n"},{"number":1026,"text":"for the current gap on the exact basis\", refined by cause as A8 refines\n"},{"number":1027,"text":"`block`, on `initial_selection` and after a closure decision alike. A\n"},{"number":1028,"text":"closure decision it follows (`post_retry`, `post_repair`, `post_reenter`,\n"},{"number":1029,"text":"`post_block`, `post_reprice`) keeps its own value for the edge predicate\n"},{"number":1030,"text":"under A8 below and is evidence for the selection, not its result; neither\n"},{"number":1031,"text":"value counts toward the other's predicate\n"},{"number":1032,"text":"([FOUNDATIONS §4 Conjunction of results](FOUNDATIONS.md#conjunction-of-results)).\n"},{"number":1033,"text":"An earlier result stands for a no-action only where it decided this same\n"},{"number":1034,"text":"predicate on the current basis. In the rule above, `reprice or block` and\n"},{"number":1035,"text":"`block with typed no-action disposition` name typed no-actions of\n"},{"number":1036,"text":"`evaluate_next`, not closure decisions, which only `evaluate_action` makes\n"},{"number":1037,"text":"(A4a); on `post_reprice` the no-action is `no_action(undeclared)`, naming\n"},{"number":1038,"text":"the outcome or bound the reprice found undeclared and the re-entry that\n"},{"number":1039,"text":"would declare it (P5, P6):\n"},{"number":1040,"text":"\n"},{"number":1041,"text":"| Typed no-action | Core value | Ground |\n"},{"number":1042,"text":"|---|---|---|\n"},{"number":1043,"text":"| `no_action(undeclared)` | out_of_frame | the gap needs an action that the admitted catalogue does not publish, or a relation, capability or authority that no admitted basis declares (A3); the value names what is missing and its owner, as for `block(undeclared)` |\n"},{"number":1044,"text":"| `no_action(basis)` | invalid_basis | no published action is lawful on the current bound outcome, catalogue and policy, which cannot support the gap, as for `block(basis)` |\n"},{"number":1045,"text":"\n"},{"number":1046,"text":"The typed no-action stays beside its core value as the domain outcome,\n"},{"number":1047,"text":"and producing the next-action projection establishes availability only\n"},{"number":1048,"text":"([ACTIVATION §3 Construction availability](ACTIVATION.md#construction-availability)).\n"},{"number":1049,"text":"Where the graph is complete and no action follows a closed edge, nothing\n"},{"number":1050,"text":"is refused and no selection determination is made: the empty selection is\n"},{"number":1051,"text":"invocational data, and `close` keeps its own value for the edge predicate\n"},{"number":1052,"text":"under A8.\n"},{"number":1053,"text":"\n"},{"number":1054,"text":"A4a Evaluation function separation. `synthesize_model`, `eval_gap`,\n"},{"number":1055,"text":"`evaluate_next` and `evaluate_action` are separate authority functions.\n"},{"number":1056,"text":"`synthesize_model` may synthesise the current product asset model from\n"},{"number":1057,"text":"event-log-backed intent lineage, prior model and admitted product truth\n"},{"number":1058,"text":"only; it must not admit construction intent,\n"},{"number":1061,"text":"invoke work, close work or select the next action. `evaluate_action` may\n"},{"number":1062,"text":"fold admitted action evidence into ledger and closure decision only; it\n"},{"number":1063,"text":"must not select the next graph action. `evaluate_next` may bind target\n"},{"number":1064,"text":"obligations, rank lawful published actions and publish the next-action\n"},{"number":1065,"text":"projection; it must not admit construction intent or decide whether the\n"},{"number":1066,"text":"prior action closed, yielded, failed or needs repair except by consuming\n"},{"number":1067,"text":"the admitted `EdgeClosureDecision`. A module that lets one of these\n"},{"number":1068,"text":"functions exercise another's authority creates a rival control surface\n"},{"number":1069,"text":"and violates A0 (P6).\n"},{"number":1070,"text":"\n"},{"number":1071,"text":"A5 F_P freedom, F_D authority. F_P may inspect, discover, reason, draft,\n"},{"number":1072,"text":"transform and propose within the bounded action. F_P must not close the\n"},{"number":1073,"text":"edge, emit runtime events, mutate ledgers or decide re-entry. F_D, ABG and\n"},{"number":1074,"text":"product admission own durable evidence and closure truth (P0, P6). An F_P\n"},{"number":1075,"text":"worker must not be given an F_D-authored semantic extraction recipe when\n"},{"number":1076,"text":"the edge requires F_P judgment (P0, P6; provenance rc.2\n"},{"number":1077,"text":"authority_compressions/design_module_method.compressed.md, a claim no\n"},{"number":1078,"text":"rc.2 body states).\n"},{"number":1079,"text":"\n"},{"number":1080,"text":"A6 Evidence before closure. No edge closes from raw artifact presence, a\n"},{"number":1081,"text":"raw worker report, raw process success or a CLI summary. Action selection belongs to\n"},{"number":1103,"text":"`evaluate_next` over `EdgeClosureDecision` plus fresh `eval_gap` truth\n"},{"number":1104,"text":"(P6).\n"},{"number":1105,"text":"\n"},{"number":1106,"text":"A8 Closure decision is a sum type. The closure decision vocabulary is\n"},{"number":1107,"text":"closed (P5, P6):\n"},{"number":1108,"text":"\n"},{"number":1109,"text":"```text\n"},{"number":1110,"text":"close\n"},{"number":1111,"text":"yield\n"},{"number":1112,"text":"retry\n"},{"number":1113,"text":"repair\n"},{"number":1114,"text":"re-enter\n"},{"number":1115,"text":"reprice\n"},{"number":1116,"text":"block\n"},{"number":1117,"text":"```\n"},{"number":1118,"text":"\n"},{"number":1119,"text":"Each disposition is replay-visible and must carry basis and reason refs\n"},{"number":1120,"text":"sufficient to reproduce the decision (P5, P6).\n"},{"number":1121,"text":"\n"},{"number":1122,"text":"Mapping of the closure decision to the core result algebra, new in this\n"},{"number":1123,"text":"corpus. Purpose: [FOUNDATIONS §4 Results](FOUNDATIONS.md#results) states\n"},{"number":1124,"text":"every outcome in the core or in a refinement that maps each refined value\n"},{"number":1125,"text":"to exactly one core value and adds no core value. Material relation: a\n"},{"number":1126,"text":"composite over the edge joins each closure decision as one core value, so\n"},{"number":1127,"text":"a failure, an undecided closure and a needed re-entry stay distinct (P5,\n"},{"number":1128,"text":"P6). The closure decision is `evaluate_action`'s result over admitted\n"},{"number":1129,"text":"evidence, mapped for the predicate \"the edge's obligations are fulfilled\n"},{"number":1130,"text":"on the exact basis\". The bounded action it judges has its own step result\n"},{"number":1131,"text":"under [ACTIVATION §3 Construction availability](ACTIVATION.md#construction-availability)\n"},{"number":1132,"text":"for \"a candidate is available\", and neither value counts toward the\n"},{"number":1133,"text":"other's predicate\n"},{"number":1134,"text":"([FOUNDATIONS §4 Conjunction of results](FOUNDATIONS.md#conjunction-of-results)).\n"},{"number":1135,"text":"Each decision keeps its own vocabulary and its basis and reason refs\n"},{"number":1136,"text":"beside its core value (P5, P6).\n"},{"number":1137,"text":"\n"},{"number":1138,"text":"Refined by cause. Where a decision would map to one of two core values\n"},{"number":1139,"text":"according to its cause, the cause is part of the refined value, written\n"},{"number":1140,"text":"in parentheses as in `re-enter(basis)`, and each refined value maps once\n"},{"number":1141,"text":"(FOUNDATIONS §4 Results, Refinements). The cause is read from the\n"},{"number":1142,"text":"decision's basis and reason refs, which A8 requires to be sufficient to\n"},{"number":1143,"text":"reproduce it. The seven decisions of A8 stay the closed vocabulary; a\n"},{"number":1144,"text":"refined value names a decision and its cause and adds no decision (P5,\n"},{"number":1145,"text":"P6). The causes are:\n"},{"number":1146,"text":"\n"},{"number":1147,"text":"- `retry(defeated)`: admissible evidence defeats the edge for this attempt\n"},{"number":1148,"text":"  while the same action stays lawful; `retry(undecided)`: every other\n"},{"number":1149,"text":"  retry, as after a liveness interruption, which alone never gives\n"},{"number":1150,"text":"  `retry(defeated)` (A10).\n"},{"number":1151,"text":"- `repair(implementation)` and `repair(proof)`: the edge's governing\n"},{"number":1152,"text":"  relation, basis and target binding stay coherent, and a local defect,\n"},{"number":1153,"text":"  in the implementation or in its proof, keeps the edge from closing, so\n"},{"number":1154,"text":"  a repair of the same edge is the lawful next action (A11).\n"},{"number":1155,"text":"  `repair(implementation)`: admitted evidence shows the implementation\n"},{"number":1156,"text":"  failing an edge obligation; `repair(proof)`: every other repair, the\n"},{"number":1157,"text":"  defect being in the proof.\n"},{"number":1158,"text":"- `re-enter(undeclared)` and `block(undeclared)`: the cause is a relation,\n"},{"number":1159,"text":"  capability or authority that no admitted basis declares;\n"},{"number":1160,"text":"  `re-enter(basis)` and `block(basis)`: every other cause, the current\n"},{"number":1161,"text":"  basis being unable to support the edge, as when it is stale, substituted\n"},{"number":1162,"text":"  or unrepairable. A decision with both causes records both and takes the\n"},{"number":1163,"text":"  `basis` value, as\n"},{"number":1164,"text":"  [ACTIVATION §3 Construction availability](ACTIVATION.md#construction-availability)\n"},{"number":1165,"text":"  decides for `re_entry_requested`.\n"},{"number":1166,"text":"\n"},{"number":1167,"text":"| Decision | Core value | Ground |\n"},{"number":1168,"text":"|---|---|---|\n"},{"number":1169,"text":"| `close` | satisfied | the ledger's admitted evidence fulfils the edge's obligations on the exact basis |\n"},{"number":1170,"text":"| `yield` | indeterminate | the edge stays lawful and the admitted evidence, progress or a waiting state, does not yet decide its obligations; the replay-visible resume basis names what would decide; no failure is classified, as A9 requires |\n"},{"number":1171,"text":"| `retry(defeated)` | falsified | the evidence that defeats the edge for this attempt is the counterexample; A9 separates this failure-classified re-attempt from yield |\n"},{"number":1172,"text":"| `retry(undecided)` | indeterminate | the attempt ended without evidence that decides the edge's obligations; the decision's reason refs name the re-attempt that would decide |\n"},{"number":1173,"text":"| `repair(implementation)` | falsified | the failing obligation row is the counterexample (P5, P6) |\n"},{"number":1174,"text":"| `repair(proof)` | indeterminate | the defect is in the proof, so the admitted evidence cannot decide the obligation; the decision's reason refs name the repaired proof that would decide (P5, P6) |\n"},{"number":1175,"text":"| `re-enter(undeclared)` | out_of_frame | the edge cannot close on its current basis because an upstream asset or governing relation must change, and the change needs a relation, capability or authority that no admitted basis declares; the decision names it and its owner |\n"},{"number":1176,"text":"| `re-enter(basis)` | invalid_basis | the edge cannot close on its current basis because an upstream asset or governing relation must change, and that basis cannot support the edge |\n"},{"number":1177,"text":"| `reprice` | out_of_frame | further work needs a selected outcome or bound that no admitted basis declares; the decision names it, its owner and the constitutional re-entry it requests, which is the reconfiguration pressure of out_of_frame ([FOUNDATIONS §4 Results](FOUNDATIONS.md#results), Out of frame or indeterminate) |\n"},{"number":1178,"text":"| `block(undeclared)` | out_of_frame | `evaluate_action` decides that the action just taken leaves the edge unable to proceed, because it needs a relation, capability or authority that no admitted basis declares |\n"},{"number":1179,"text":"| `block(basis)` | invalid_basis | `evaluate_action` decides that the action just taken leaves the edge unable to proceed on its current basis, which cannot support it |\n"},{"number":1180,"text":"\n"},{"number":1181,"text":"Closure decisions and dispositions. Invocation rule\n"},{"number":1681,"text":"\n"},{"number":1682,"text":"When an agent or designer is told `using ODD, build this product`, the\n"},{"number":1683,"text":"expected sequence is (P2, over admitted\n"},{"number":1707,"text":"    constructive history, including the structural coherence rule between\n"},{"number":1708,"text":"    the read model and its source Discussion state and mutability\n"},{"number":118,"text":"\n"},{"number":119,"text":"A post is mutable while it is part of an open discussion. Use `Status` to\n"},{"number":120,"text":"make the discussion state explicit (P3, P4b; rc.2\n"},{"number":121,"text":"#discussion-state-and-mutability):\n"},{"number":122,"text":"\n"},{"number":123,"text":"- `Draft`: the author is still shaping the post;\n"},{"number":124,"text":"- `Open`: the post is published into an active discussion; and\n"},{"number":125,"text":"- `Closed`: the discussion has concluded.\n"},{"number":126,"text":"\n"},{"number":127,"text":"While a post is `Draft` or `Open`, the same post is updated when the\n"},{"number":128,"text":"change still belongs to the same bounded discussion: correcting\n"},{"number":129,"text":"mistakes, incorporating replies, refining recommendations, repricing\n"},{"number":130,"text":"confidence, or adding new evidence on the same subject (P3). Constitutional-set sufficiency\n"},{"number":195,"text":"\n"},{"number":196,"text":"A Constitutional Set is the complete set of governing documents selected\n"},{"number":197,"text":"for a product, not a prescribed number of files. Collectively the selected\n"},{"number":198,"text":"set makes five things recoverable for the governed scope (P2, P1; rc.2\n"},{"number":199,"text":"#constitutional-set-sufficiency):\n"},{"number":200,"text":"\n"},{"number":201,"text":"- axioms: irreducible starting truths, invariants and refusal conditions;\n"},{"number":202,"text":"- ontology: the kinds of things treated as real and their material\n"},{"number":203,"text":"  identities, relations, boundaries and lifecycle;\n"},{"number":204,"text":"- epistemology: how claims become knowable or acceptable, including\n"},{"number":205,"text":"  admissible evidence, observation, uncertainty, falsification and\n"},{"number":206,"text":"  decision authority;\n"},{"number":207,"text":"- taxonomy: the classifications and kind distinctions that prevent\n"},{"number":208,"text":"  category collapse; and\n"},{"number":209,"text":"- semantics: the governed meaning of terms, relations, states, operations\n"},{"number":210,"text":"  and outcomes.\n"},{"number":211,"text":"\n"},{"number":212,"text":"These are sufficiency dimensions of the complete set, not filename classes,\n"},{"number":213,"text":"required folders or a one-document-per-dimension partition. One document\n"},{"number":214,"text":"may cover several dimensions and one dimension may be distributed across\n"},{"number":215,"text":"several owning documents (P2).\n"},{"number":216,"text":"\n"},{"number":217,"text":"`constitution.stdo.basis` locates the complete selected STDO constitutional\n"},{"number":218,"text":"set; `constitution.additional_authorities` locates every additional\n"},{"number":219,"text":"constitutional set or source. Shared installed release basis and toolchain manager\n"},{"number":234,"text":"\n"},{"number":235,"text":"Within the product-definition-routing context an Installed Release Basis\n"},{"number":236,"text":"is the pair (P3; rc.2\n"},{"number":237,"text":"#shared-installed-release-basis-and-toolchain-manager):\n"},{"number":238,"text":"\n"},{"number":239,"text":"```text\n"},{"number":240,"text":"(immutable stdo://releases/<cut>/ URI, installed-manifest SHA-256)\n"},{"number":241,"text":"```\n"},{"number":242,"text":"\n"},{"number":243,"text":"The URI identifies one immutable annotated RC cut. The digest identifies\n"},{"number":244,"text":"the deterministic installed-release manifest, which binds the cut's tag\n"},{"number":245,"text":"object, commit, repository tree, standards subtree, exact standards\n"},{"number":246,"text":"inventory, member bytes and admitted auxiliary payloads. The manifest shape\n"},{"number":247,"text":"is owned by `schemas/installed-release-manifest.schema.json`. The manifest\n"},{"number":248,"text":"is transport and integrity evidence, not a replacement constitution (P6).\n"},{"number":249,"text":"\n"},{"number":250,"text":"An Adoption Plan is the read-only, deterministic acceptance object that\n"},{"number":251,"text":"binds one current Product Definition's identity and bytes to its current\n"},{"number":252,"text":"basis and to one resolved immutable target's cut, annotated tag object,\n"},{"number":253,"text":"commit, tree and installed-manifest digest. Its SHA-256 is the\n"},{"number":254,"text":"operator-presentable acceptance token. Possession of the token alone grants\n"},{"number":255,"text":"no authority; a mismatch proves that the presented subject is no longer the\n"},{"number":256,"text":"subject being mutated (P6, P3). A Fleet Adoption Plan binds an authorised\n"},{"number":257,"text":"fleet root and the complete ordered set of per-definition plan digests\n"},{"number":258,"text":"under the same law.\n"},{"number":259,"text":"\n"},{"number":260,"text":"Plan hashing covers the acceptance object only: local installation status\n"},{"number":261,"text":"and machine-local install paths are excluded. The canonical bytes are UTF-8\n"},{"number":262,"text":"JSON with recursively sorted object keys, two-space indentation, LF line\n"},{"number":263,"text":"endings and one final LF. Fleet order is deterministic discovery-path\n"},{"number":264,"text":"order. These rules make a presented digest independently reproducible\n"},{"number":265,"text":"rather than an opaque session token (P3, P6).\n"},{"number":266,"text":"\n"},{"number":267,"text":"The STDO Toolchain Manager is the bounded executable realisation that (P2,\n"},{"number":268,"text":"P3):\n"},{"number":269,"text":"\n"},{"number":270,"text":"- installs each immutable cut once in a shared, versioned release store;\n"},{"number":271,"text":"- resolves logical `stdo://releases/...` URIs to verified installed bytes;\n"},{"number":272,"text":"- inventories and verifies complete installed distributions;\n"},{"number":273,"text":"- synchronises the exact basis already selected by a Product Definition;\n"},{"number":274,"text":"- emits an exact digest-bound adoption plan from a mutable version-line\n"},{"number":275,"text":"  selector and performs adoption only when that plan is explicitly\n"},{"number":276,"text":"  accepted;\n"},{"number":277,"text":"- manages the stable marker-bounded bootstrap in declared agent files; and\n"},{"number":278,"text":"- applies those operations to an explicitly discovered fleet of Product\n"},{"number":279,"text":"  Definitions.\n"},{"number":280,"text":"\n"},{"number":281,"text":"The store may live at any machine-local path. Its registry maps logical\n"},{"number":282,"text":"release URIs to installed paths and manifest digests and owns neither\n"},{"number":283,"text":"Product selection nor method meaning. A consumer repository therefore needs\n"},{"number":284,"text":"no project-local standards copy and no prescribed folder structure, and\n"},{"number":285,"text":"several Product Definitions on one machine may select different installed\n"},{"number":286,"text":"cuts concurrently (P3, P6). Neither is operative authority.\n"},{"number":298,"text":"`constitution.stdo.basis.uri` and `constitution.stdo.basis.manifest_sha256`\n"},{"number":299,"text":"are the sole authored STDO selection for that Product Definition (P6, P3).\n"},{"number":300,"text":"\n"},{"number":301,"text":"The manager observes these command boundaries (P6, P5):\n"},{"number":302,"text":"\n"},{"number":303,"text":"- `install` admits one explicitly named immutable RC cut without changing\n"},{"number":304,"text":"  any Product Definition;\n"},{"number":305,"text":"- `sync` installs and verifies only the exact basis already selected by\n"},{"number":306,"text":"  the Product Definition and never consults its mutable selector;\n"},{"number":307,"text":"- `adopt --dry-run` resolves the annotated selector and immutable RC, and\n"},{"number":308,"text":"  presents the target cut, tag object, commit, tree, manifest digest,\n"},{"number":309,"text":"  current Product-Definition byte digest and a deterministic\n"},{"number":310,"text":"  adoption-plan digest, without mutation;\n"},{"number":311,"text":"- mutating `adopt` requires that exact prior plan digest as explicit\n"},{"number":312,"text":"  acceptance, re-derives it before installation, installs only the bound\n"},{"number":313,"text":"  target, rechecks the Product Definition bytes, and atomically changes\n"},{"number":314,"text":"  only its basis and its basis-relative schema locator; any drift refuses\n"},{"number":315,"text":"  mutation;\n"},{"number":316,"text":"- `status`, `verify`, `list`, `manifest` and `resolve` are read-only with\n"},{"number":317,"text":"  respect to Product Definitions;
